# Escargot on hypothesis statements (EvoAge graph)

## 1. Systematic profiling reveals betaine as an exercise mimetic for geroprotection

**Hypothesis:** Betaine supplementation promotes healthy aging and inhibits cellular senescence. By acting as an exercise mimetic, betaine inhibits TBK1 activity. This targeted inhibition promotes a reduction in systemic inflammation and promotes cellular repair, effectively delaying age-related physical decline

```cypher
MATCH (ce:ChemicalEntity)-[:ChemicalEntity_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE ce.name_lower CONTAINS 'betaine' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE p.name_lower CONTAINS 'healthy aging' RETURN g.id AS gene LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE p.name_lower CONTAINS 'healthy aging' RETURN g.id AS geneSymbol LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE p.name_lower CONTAINS 'healthy aging' OR p.name_lower CONTAINS 'delayed age-related physical decline' RETURN g.id AS gene LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (p:Phenotype)-[:Disease_Phenotype]-(g:Gene) WHERE p.name_lower CONTAINS 'healthy aging' RETURN g.id AS gene LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE p.name_lower CONTAINS 'healthy aging' OR p.name_lower CONTAINS 'delayed age-related physical decline' RETURN g.id AS gene ;; MATCH (p:Phenotype)-[:Gene_Phenotype]-(g:Gene) WHERE p.name_lower CONTAINS 'healthy aging' RETURN g.id AS gene LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE p.name_lower CONTAINS 'delayed age-related physical decline' RETURN g.id AS gene LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE p.name_lower CONTAINS 'delayed age-related physical decline' RETURN g.id AS geneSymbol LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (p:Phenotype)-[:Disease_Phenotype]-(d:Disease)-[:Disease_Gene]-(g:Gene) WHERE p.name_lower CONTAINS 'delayed age-related physical decline' RETURN g.id AS gene LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (p:Protein)-[:Protein_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE p.id_lower = 'tbk1' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (p:Protein)-[r:Protein_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE p.id_lower = 'tbk1' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'cellular senescence' RETURN g.id AS gene LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'aging' RETURN g.id AS gene LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'inflammatory response' RETURN g.id AS gene LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'systemic inflammation' RETURN g.id AS gene LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'systemic inflammation' RETURN g.id AS geneSymbol LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (p:Protein)-[:Protein_Protein]-(interacting:Protein) WHERE p.id_lower = 'tbk1' RETURN interacting.id AS protein LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (p1:Protein)-[r:Protein_Protein]-(p2:Protein) WHERE p1.id_lower = 'tbk1' RETURN p2.id AS interacting_protein LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (p1:Protein)-[:Protein_Protein]-(p2:Protein) WHERE p1.id_lower = 'tbk1' RETURN p2.id AS protein LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (p1:Protein)-[:Protein_Protein]-(p2:Protein) WHERE toLower(p1.id_lower) = 'tbk1' RETURN p2.id AS protein LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (p1:Protein)-[:Protein_Protein]-(p2:Protein) WHERE p1.id_lower = 'tbk1' RETURN p2.id AS interacting_protein LIMIT 25
```

## 2. Stress granule clearance mediated by V-ATPase-interacting protein NCOA7 mitigates ovarian aging

**Hypothesis:** Therapeutic delivery of NCOA7 mRNA promotes the autophagic degradation of stress granules and inhibits oxidative stress in human granulosa cells. By efficiently clearing these granules, this targeted intervention promotes ovarian resilience and strongly inhibits cellular senescence, thereby successfully delaying ovarian aging and extending female reproductive longevity.

```cypher
MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'ncoa7' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (p:Protein)-[:Protein_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE p.id_lower = 'ncoa7' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE g.id_lower = 'ncoa7' RETURN p.name AS phenotype LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_AnatomicalEntity]-(a:AnatomicalEntity) WHERE g.id_lower = 'ncoa7' RETURN a.name AS anatomical_entity LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[r:Gene_Disease]-(d:Disease) WHERE g.id_lower = 'ncoa7' RETURN d.name AS disease LIMIT 25
```

The final verification result shows all relevant lists are empty and `chain_confirmed` is `False`.

However, none of the extracted data support the claim: no autophagy or stress granule processes, no ovarian phenotypes, no ovarian anatomical entities, and no ovarian diseases were found. Therefore, the mechanistic chain is not confirmed.

## 3. Deletion of METTL14, a key methylation regulator, attenuates vascular ageing

**Hypothesis:** Targeted knockdown of METTL14 inhibits the stabilization of TLR4 mRNA, which inhibits chronic vascular inflammation. This molecular intervention inhibits endothelial cell senescence and arterial stiffness, and ultimately promotes healthy vascular aging and cardiovascular resilience in older individuals.

```cypher
MATCH (g:Gene)-[r:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'mettl14' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'stabilization' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'tlr4' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'chronic vascular inflammation' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[r:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'tlr4' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'chronic vascular inflammation' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'tlr4' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'chronic vascular inflammation' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'mettl14' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'endothelial cell senescence' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'mettl14' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'endothelial cell senescence' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[r:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'mettl14' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'endothelial cell senescence' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE g.id_lower = 'mettl14' AND p.name_lower CONTAINS 'arterial stiffness' RETURN p.name AS phenotype ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'mettl14' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE g.id_lower = 'tlr4' AND p.name_lower CONTAINS 'arterial stiffness' RETURN p.name AS phenotype ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[r:Gene_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE g.id_lower = 'tlr4' AND p.name_lower CONTAINS 'arterial stiffness' RETURN p.name AS phenotype ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE g.id_lower = 'tlr4' AND p.name_lower CONTAINS 'arterial stiffness' RETURN g.id AS gene
```

Steps 3 and 4 verify the effects on endothelial cell senescence and arterial stiffness. Step 5 looks for the outcomes of healthy vascular aging and cardiovascular resilience. ## 4. NRF1-mediated innate immune response drives inflammaging

**Hypothesis:** Targeted knockdown of NRF1 inhibits the TBK1/IRF3 innate immune signaling pathway, which directly inhibits the senescence-associated secretory phenotype (SASP). This reduction in systemic inflammation inhibits cellular senescence and promotes healthy aging, effectively extending lifespan and mitigating age-related multi-organ deterioration.

## 5. Panaxatriol exerts anti-senescence effects and alleviates osteoarthritis and cartilage repair fibrosis by targeting UFL1

**Hypothesis:** Targeted treatment with panaxatriol promotes UFL1 activity, which directly inhibits chondrocyte senescence and inhibits inflammatory SASP release. By regulating these pathways, the intervention inhibits abnormal fibrocartilage formation and promotes healthy cartilage repair, effectively delaying the degenerative progression of osteoarthritis.

```cypher
MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'ufl1' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'chondrocyte senescence' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[r:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'ufl1' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'chondrocyte senescence' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; MATCH (bp1:BiologicalProcess)-[r]-(bp2:BiologicalProcess) WHERE bp1.name_lower CONTAINS 'chondrocyte senescence' AND bp2.name_lower CONTAINS 'inflammatory sasp release' RETURN bp2.name AS process ;; MATCH (bp1:BiologicalProcess)-[r]-(bp2:BiologicalProcess) WHERE bp1.name_lower CONTAINS 'chondrocyte senescence' AND bp2.name_lower CONTAINS 'inflammatory sasp release' RETURN bp1.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (bp1:BiologicalProcess)-[:BiologicalProcess_BiologicalProcess]-(bp2:BiologicalProcess)  WHERE bp1.name_lower CONTAINS 'chondrocyte senescence' AND bp2.name_lower CONTAINS 'inflammatory sasp release'  RETURN bp2.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (bp1:BiologicalProcess)-[r]-(bp2:BiologicalProcess) WHERE bp1.name_lower CONTAINS 'chondrocyte senescence'   AND bp2.name_lower CONTAINS 'inflammatory sasp release' RETURN bp1.name AS process ;; MATCH (ce:ChemicalEntity)-[:Gene_ChemicalEntity]-(g:Gene) WHERE ce.name_lower CONTAINS 'panaxatriol' AND g.id_lower = 'ufl1' RETURN ce.name AS chemical_entity ;; MATCH (ce:ChemicalEntity)-[:Gene_ChemicalEntity]-(g:Gene) WHERE ce.name_lower CONTAINS 'panaxatriol' AND g.id_lower = 'ufl1' RETURN ce.name AS chemical_name ;; MATCH (ce:ChemicalEntity)-[r:Gene_ChemicalEntity]-(g:Gene) WHERE toLower(ce.name_lower) CONTAINS 'panaxatriol' AND toLower(g.id_lower) = 'ufl1' RETURN ce.name AS chemical_entity ;; MATCH (ce:ChemicalEntity)-[:Gene_ChemicalEntity]-(g:Gene) WHERE ce.name_lower CONTAINS 'panaxatriol' AND g.id_lower = 'ufl1' RETURN ce.name AS chemical_name LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (d:Disease)-[:Disease_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE toLower(d.name_lower) CONTAINS 'osteoarthritis' AND toLower(p.name_lower) CONTAINS 'abnormal fibrocartilage formation' RETURN p.name AS phenotype ;; MATCH (d:Disease)-[:Disease_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE toLower(d.name_lower) CONTAINS 'osteoarthritis' AND toLower(p.name_lower) CONTAINS 'abnormal fibrocartilage formation' RETURN p.name AS phenotype LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (d:Disease)-[:Disease_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE d.name_lower CONTAINS 'osteoarthritis'    AND p.name_lower CONTAINS 'abnormal fibrocartilage formation' RETURN d.name AS disease_name ;; MATCH (d:Disease)-[:Disease_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE d.name_lower CONTAINS 'osteoarthritis'  AND p.name_lower CONTAINS 'abnormal fibrocartilage formation' RETURN p.name AS phenotype ;; MATCH (d:Disease)-[r]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE d.name_lower CONTAINS 'osteoarthritis' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'healthy cartilage repair' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; MATCH (d:Disease)-[:Disease_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE d.name_lower CONTAINS 'osteoarthritis' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'healthy cartilage repair' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; ## 6. PRDM16 Reduces Cellular Senescence by Upregulating GSTM1

**Hypothesis:** Targeted delivery of PRDM16 promotes GSTM1 transcription and promotes healthy glutathione metabolism. This enhanced antioxidant activity directly inhibits oxidative DNA damage and inhibits cellular senescence across multiple organs. Ultimately, this targeted intervention inhibits aging-related organ injury and promotes healthy organ function in older individuals.

```cypher

MATCH (d:Disease)-[:Disease_AnatomicalEntity]-(a:AnatomicalEntity) WHERE toLower(d.name_lower) CONTAINS 'aging' RETURN a.name AS anatomical_entity LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (d:Disease)-[:Disease_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE toLower(p.name_lower) CONTAINS 'aging-related organ injury' RETURN d.name AS disease LIMIT 25 ;; ## 11. Elevating cytosolic NADPH metabolism in endothelial cells ameliorates vascular aging

**Hypothesis:** Upregulating glucose-6-phosphate dehydrogenase (G6PD) promotes the cytosolic production of reduced nicotinamide adenine dinucleotide phosphate (NADPH). This targeted metabolic enhancement inhibits the pathogenic activity of histone deacetylase 3 (HDAC3), which directly inhibits endothelial cell (EC) senescence. Ultimately, sustaining this antioxidant pathway promotes vascular health and successfully delays cardiovascular aging.

## 12. Metformin inhibits nuclear egress of chromatin fragments in senescence and aging

**Hypothesis:** Metformin promotes AMP-activated protein kinase (AMPK) activity, which promotes the degradation of ALG-2-interacting protein X (ALIX). This breakdown directly inhibits the nuclear escape of cytoplasmic chromatin fragments (CCFs). Halting this egress inhibits the cyclic GMP-AMP synthase and stimulator of interferon genes (cGAS-STING) pathway, which ultimately inhibits senescence-associated inflammation and promotes overall longevity.

## 13. Targeting NAT10 alleviates colonic senescence and elderly-onset colitis by disrupting N4-acetylation of DYRK1A

**Hypothesis:** Knockdown or inhibition of the critical regulator N-acetyltransferase 10 (NAT10) inhibits mRNA N4-acetylcytidine (ac4C) modification, which inhibits the stabilization of dual specificity tyrosine-phosphorylation-regulated kinase 1A (DYRK1A) mRNA. This directly inhibits colonic epithelial cell senescence, which ultimately inhibits elderly-onset ulcerative colitis (UC) and promotes the rejuvenation of intestinal aging.

Consequently, this suppression inhibits cyclin-dependent kinase inhibitor 1A (CDKN1A) expression, which directly inhibits human umbilical vein endothelial cell senescence. ## 15. NNMT inhibition counteracts tubular senescence and fibrosis in early stages of chronic kidney disease

**Hypothesis:** Nicotinamide-N-methyltransferase (NNMT) promotes tubular epithelial cell senescence. This molecular overexpression promotes p21 association and promotes partial epithelial-to-mesenchymal transition. Consequently, this enzymatic activity promotes a pro-inflammatory microenvironment, which directly promotes kidney fibrosis. Ultimately, the biological presence of NNMT promotes renal aging features and promotes early-stage chronic kidney disease (CKD) progression.

## 16. Pyrroloquinoline Quinone Is an Effective Senomorphic Agent to Target the Pro-Inflammatory Phenotype of Senescent Cells

**Hypothesis:** Pyrroloquinoline quinone (PQQ) is a redox cofactor that inhibits the aging-related senescence-associated secretory phenotype (SASP). By directly targeting intracellular protein HSPA8, PQQ inhibits downstream SASP expression. Consequently, this senomorphic molecule inhibits stromal cancer cell malignancy and inhibits chemotherapy resistance. Ultimately, PQQ inhibits age-related organ degeneration and promotes geroprotective effects.

Consequently, this cellular pathway directly inhibits inner ear hair cell senescence and inhibits senescent cell loss. Ultimately, RONIN inhibits age-related hearing loss and promotes auditory aging health.

## 18. Hyperglutaminolysis drives senescence and aging through arginine-mTORC1 axis activation

**Hypothesis:** The biological enzyme glutaminase 1 (GLS1) promotes hyperglutaminolysis. This metabolic hyperactivation promotes arginine biosynthesis via argininosuccinate lyase (ASL). Consequently, this molecular cascade promotes persistent mTORC1 activation. This nutrient-sensing signaling directly promotes cellular senescence. Ultimately, GLS1 promotes pathological aging processes and inhibits age-related health.

## 19. Pck1 Deficiency Drives Mitochondrial Dysfunction and Cellular Senescence in Adipocytes

## 20. Targeting Endothelial KDM5A to Attenuate Aging and Ameliorate Age-Associated Metabolic Abnormalities

**Hypothesis:** The biological enzyme lysine-specific demethylase 5A (KDM5A) inhibits histone H3 lysine 4 (H3K4me3) enrichment at the fatty acid-binding protein 4 (FABP4) promoter. This epigenetic regulation inhibits active FABP4 transcription and inhibits fatty acid (FA) metabolism disorders. ## 22. Quercetin alleviates ethanol-induced hepatocyte senescence by interacting with glycinamide ribonucleotide transformylase

**Hypothesis:** The biological compound quercetin inhibits glycinamide ribonucleotide transformylase (GART) activity. This molecular interaction inhibits 5-aminoimidazole-4-carboxamide-1-β-D-ribofuranoside (AICAR) biosynthesis within the purine metabolic pathway. Consequently, this metabolic suppression inhibits the expression of senescent markers p53, p21, and p16, which directly inhibits ethanol-induced hepatocyte senescence. Ultimately, the pharmacological action of quercetin inhibits alcoholic liver disease progression and promotes hepatic cellular health.

## 23. Azithromycin Mitigates Tobacco Smoke-Induced Lung Senescence by Modulating the FOXO3A/CCND1 Signaling Pathway

**Hypothesis:** The pharmacological drug azithromycin promotes Forkhead box O3 (FOXO3A) expression and inhibits Cyclin D1 (CCND1) expression. This molecular regulation inhibits the senescence-associated secretory phenotype (SASP) and inhibits the expression of senescence markers p53 and p21. Consequently, this suppression directly inhibits bronchial epithelial cell senescence and inhibits accelerated lung aging. Ultimately, the pharmacological action of azithromycin inhibits chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD) progression and promotes pulmonary health.

## 25. Enhancing cardiomyocyte reprogramming efficiency by targeting cellular senescence is mediated via Rb1 gene

**Hypothesis:** The biological gene RB1 promotes cellular senescence during direct cardiac reprogramming. This molecular upregulation promotes the expression of senescence markers P16 and GLB1 and promotes cellular apoptosis. Consequently, this senescent state directly inhibits the expression of endogenous cardiac transcription factors GATA4 and MEF2C, which inhibits the conversion of fibroblasts into cardiomyocytes. Ultimately, the biological presence of RB1 promotes cellular barriers and inhibits cardiac repair efficiency.

```cypher
MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'rb1' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (bp:BiologicalProcess)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(g:Gene) WHERE g.id_lower IN ['gata4', 'mef2c'] RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'cdkn2a' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'glb1' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25
```

The steps used specific queries to find related processes, but the outputs for steps 6 and 7 returned empty results, indicating no direct relationship was found in the data for RB1 inhibiting reprogramming or cardiac repair.

**Answer:** The biological gene RB1 promotes cellular senescence and apoptosis, which inhibits the expression of GATA4 and MEF2C, thereby blocking fibroblast-to-cardiomyocyte conversion and reducing cardiac repair efficiency.

## 29. Oxymatrine Alleviates Atherosclerosis by Regulating Macrophage Senescence via the SIRT1-P53 Signaling Pathway

**Hypothesis:** The pharmacological compound oxymatrine (OMT) promotes SIRT1 stabilization. This molecular activation promotes P53 deacetylation and degradation. Consequently, this cascade via oxymatrine directly inhibits macrophage cellular senescence and inhibits foam cell formation. Ultimately, this anti-senescence action inhibits atherosclerosis progression and promotes cardiovascular aging health.

```cypher
MATCH (ce:ChemicalEntity)-[:ChemicalEntity_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE ce.name_lower CONTAINS 'oxymatrine' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (ce:ChemicalEntity)-[:ChemicalEntity_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE ce.name_lower CONTAINS 'oxymatrine' RETURN ce.name AS chemical LIMIT 1 ;; MATCH (bp1:BiologicalProcess)-[:BiologicalProcess_BiologicalProcess]-(bp2:BiologicalProcess) WHERE bp1.name_lower CONTAINS 'p53 deacetylation' AND bp2.name_lower CONTAINS 'p53 degradation' RETURN bp1.name AS process ;; MATCH (bp1:BiologicalProcess)-[r]-(g1:Gene) WHERE bp1.name_lower CONTAINS 'p53 deacetylation' AND g1.id_lower = 'tp53' MATCH (bp2:BiologicalProcess)-[r2]-(g2:Gene) WHERE bp2.name_lower CONTAINS 'p53 degradation' AND g2.id_lower = 'sirt1' RETURN bp1.name AS process1, bp2.name AS process2 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'sirt1' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'foam cell formation' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[r:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'sirt1' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'foam cell formation' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; ## 30. KDM4 Orchestrates Epigenomic Remodeling of Senescent Cells and Potentiates the Senescence-Associated Secretory Phenotype

**Hypothesis:** The targeted inhibition of KDM4 promotes H3K9/H3K36 methylation. This epigenetic regulation inhibits chromatin accessibility and inhibits the senescence-associated secretory phenotype (SASP). Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits pro-senescence microenvironment signaling and promotes cancer cell apoptosis. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits age-related pathologies and promotes healthy organismal aging.

This cascade inhibits pro-senescence microenvironment signaling and promotes cancer cell apoptosis, ultimately inhibiting age-related pathologies and promoting healthy organismal aging.

## 31. Transcription factor MEOX1 accelerates pulmonary fibrosis by regulating mitophagy and senescence

**Hypothesis:** The targeted knockdown of MEOX1 inhibits connective tissue growth factor (CTGF) expression. This molecular suppression promotes mitophagy and inhibits collagen production. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits lung fibroblast cellular senescence and inhibits fibroblast over-activation. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits idiopathic pulmonary fibrosis progression and promotes pulmonary aging health.

```cypher
MATCH (p:Protein)-[:Protein_Disease]-(d:Disease) WHERE p.name_lower CONTAINS 'connective tissue growth factor' RETURN p.name AS protein LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'connective tissue growth factor' RETURN g.id AS geneSymbol LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[r:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'meox1' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'ctgf' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'meox1' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'ctgf' RETURN g.id AS gene ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[r:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'meox1' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'ctgf expression' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'meox1' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'ctgf' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; The thought process involves a stepwise approach to identify and link genes, biological processes, and phenotypes related to the described cascade.  

Step 1: Extract genes and biological processes associated with MDA5 (IFIH1), including those it positively regulates or inhibits.  
Step 2: Extract biological processes associated with HSF1, focusing on activation, proteostasis, and metabolic fitness.  
Step 3: Search for phenotypes related to hematopoietic stem cell senescence and quiescence, and check for links to IFIH1 and HSF1.  
Step 4: Look for biological processes named "hematopoietic aging" and "immune system health" and find any connections to IFIH1 or HSF1.  
Step 5: Check for protein-protein interactions between IFIH1 and HSF1, and between inflammatory signaling components and HSF1.  
Step 6: Compile all extracted data into a structured cascade linking MDA5 inhibition to HSF1 activation, proteostasis, metabolic fitness, senescence inhibition, and immune health.  

The final output is a compiled dictionary containing all the extracted genes, processes, phenotypes, and interactions.  

**Answer:** The targeted inhibition of MDA5 promotes HSF1 activation and inhibits inflammatory signaling, which promotes cellular proteostasis and metabolic fitness, directly inhibits hematopoietic stem cell senescence, promotes cellular quiescence, and ultimately inhibits hematopoietic aging and promotes immune system health.

## 35. Targeting KAT8 alleviates vascular senescence by modulating the INHBA/TGF-β pathway

**Hypothesis:** The epigenetic enzyme KAT8 inhibits the INHBA/TGF-β/P15 signaling axis. This targeted molecular regulation inhibits age-related cellular pathways. Consequently, this cascade directly inhibits endothelial cellular senescence and promotes vascular endothelial homeostasis. Ultimately, this anti-senescence action inhibits vascular aging processes and promotes cardiovascular health.

## 36. The HIF-1α Pathway Regulates Satellite Cell Fate During Aging Through Histone Lactylation

**Hypothesis:** The pharmacological agent roxadustat promotes HIF-1α signaling and glycolytic metabolism. This metabolic activation promotes intracellular lactate production and promotes global histone lactylation. Consequently, this epigenetic cascade directly inhibits satellite cell senescence and promotes a regenerative quiescent state. Ultimately, this anti-senescence action promotes myogenic performance and inhibits aging-associated sarcopenia.

## 37. DEL-1 is an Endogenous Senolytic Protein that Inhibits Senescence-Associated Bone Loss

**Hypothesis:** The endogenous protein DEL-1 promotes the β3 integrin/CD73/adenosine/p38 MAPK/BCL-2 signaling pathway. This molecular activation promotes cellular apoptosis and promotes macrophage-mediated clearance. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits bone marrow mesenchymal stromal cell (BM-MSC) senescence and inhibits senescent cell accumulation. Ultimately, this senolytic action inhibits age-related bone loss and promotes skeletal aging health.

## 38. HSPA12B Protects Against Age-Related Endothelial Cell Senescence by Regulating STING Degradation

**Hypothesis:** The endothelial protein HSPA12B promotes XBP1 transcriptional activity. This molecular activation promotes SEL1L/HRD1 expression and inhibits STING pathway activation via degradation. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits endothelial cellular senescence and promotes endothelial homeostasis. Ultimately, this anti-senescence mechanism inhibits age-related cardiomyopathy and promotes cardiovascular aging health

The thought process involves breaking down the described biological cascade into individual steps, using a knowledge extraction tool to query relationships between entities (genes, proteins, biological processes, phenotypes, diseases) at each step. The goal is to verify each link in the chain—from HSPA12B promoting a biological process, to that process affecting SEL1L/HRD1, to their inhibition of STING, to the downstream effects on senescence, homeostasis, cardiomyopathy, and cardiovascular health. The outputs from each step are collected and synthesized into a full cascade to confirm the entire pathway.

**Answer:**  
The cascade is confirmed as follows: HSPA12B promotes sprouting angiogenesis (biological process). This process promotes SEL1L and HRD1 expression. SEL1L/HRD1 inhibit STING pathway activation via degradation. STING inhibition directly inhibits endothelial cellular senescence. This anti-senescence mechanism promotes endothelial homeostasis, inhibits age-related cardiomyopathy, and promotes cardiovascular aging health.

## 39. ERRα-NOS2-mediated citrulline metabolism attenuates tubular epithelial cell senescence in diabetic kidney disease

**Hypothesis:** The transcriptional regulator ERRα promotes NOS2 transcription. This molecular activation promotes citrulline metabolism and production. Consequently, this targeted metabolic cascade directly inhibits tubular epithelial cell senescence and inhibits the senescence-associated secretory phenotype (SASP). Ultimately, this anti-senescence mechanism inhibits diabetic kidney disease progression and promotes renal aging health.

## 40. ERC accumulation depletes Sir2 from rDNA and induces cellular senescence by rDNA destabilization

**Hypothesis:** The targeted inhibition of Gcn5 inhibits histone H3K14 acetylation. This epigenetic suppression inhibits bidirectional promoter E-pro non-coding RNA transcription. Consequently, this cascade directly promotes ribosomal DNA (rDNA) stability and inhibits cellular senescence. Ultimately, this anti-senescence action inhibits the cellular aging process and promotes organismal lifespan.

## 41. FUNDC1 deficiency aggravates endothelial senescence and retinal dysfunction

**Hypothesis:** The mitophagy receptor FUNDC1 promotes mitochondrial autophagy. This molecular activation inhibits reactive oxygen species and promotes tight junction protein expression. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits retinal endothelial cellular senescence and inhibits vascular permeability. Ultimately, this anti-senescence action inhibits age-related retinal degeneration and promotes visual aging health.

```cypher

## 42. Pathological aging is alleviated by neutralization of the autophagy-repressive tissue hormone DBI/ACBP

**Hypothesis:** The targeted neutralization of the tissue hormone DBI/ACBP inhibits CDKN1A/p21 marker expression and promotes protective metabolic gene signatures. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits multi-organ cellular senescence and inhibits tissue fibrosis. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits pathological aging progression and promotes organismal healthspan and systemic physiological function.

## 43. Suppression of Ciliogenesis Alleviates Cellular Senescence via AKT Signaling in Gingival Aging

**Hypothesis:** The targeted suppression of primary ciliogenesis promotes AKT signaling activation and inhibits FOXO1 activity. This molecular regulation promotes DNA repair mechanisms and inhibits DNA damage. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits gingival fibroblast cellular senescence and inhibits tissue fibrosis. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits aging-related gingival diseases and promotes periodontal health.

```cypher
MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'primary ciliogenesis' RETURN g.id AS gene LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[r:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess)  WHERE bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'primary ciliogenesis'  RETURN g.id AS gene LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'primary ciliogenesis' RETURN g.id AS geneSymbol LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[r:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess)  WHERE bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'primary ciliogenesis'  RETURN g.id AS gene_symbol  LIMIT 25
```

Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits idiopathic pulmonary fibrosis and promotes respiratory aging health.

## 45. Human collagen III mRNA therapy for effective skin rejuvenation

**Hypothesis:** The mRNA-based therapeutic hCOL3A1 promotes the localized production of functional collagen III and inhibits oxidative stress. This molecular regulation promotes fibroblast proliferation and inhibits cellular apoptosis. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits dermal cellular senescence and promotes skin barrier function. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits UVB-induced skin photoaging and promotes dermal regenerative health.

This molecular regulation promotes cellular homeostasis and inhibits proinflammatory cytokine release within the cardiac microenvironment. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits premature cardiac aging and promotes structural integrity. Ultimately, this anti-senescence mechanism inhibits arrhythmogenic cardiomyopathy progression and promotes cardiovascular health.

## 47. ABI3BP deficiency alleviates Ang II-induced VSMC senescence through the Nrf2 signalling pathway

**Hypothesis:** The targeted downregulation of ABI3BP promotes Nrf2-mediated antioxidant defense pathways and inhibits the secretion of senescence-associated inflammatory cytokines. This molecular regulation inhibits vascular collagen accumulation and promotes structural integrity. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits vascular smooth muscle cell senescence and inhibits pathological intimal thickening. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits vascular aging progression and promotes cardiovascular health.

## 49. 18β-glycyrrhetinic acid mitigates lipotoxicity-induced premature senescence of tubular epithelial cells by activating SIRT1-TFEB signaling

**Hypothesis:** The pharmacological agent 18β-glycyrrhetinic acid promotes the SIRT1-TFEB-LC3 signaling axis and inhibits PPARγ activity. This metabolic regulation promotes renal lipophagy and inhibits intracellular lipotoxicity. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits tubular cellular senescence and inhibits renal interstitial fibrosis. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits chronic kidney disease progression and promotes renal metabolic health.

## 50. Limonin mitigates hepatic senescence and fibrosis in MASH mice by targeting STAT3 to inhibit Galectin-3/mTORC1 signaling

**Hypothesis:** The pharmacological agent limonin inhibits STAT3 activity. This molecular regulation inhibits galectin-3 expression and inhibits mTORC1 pathway activation. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits hepatic cellular senescence and inhibits hepatic tissue fibrosis. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits metabolic dysfunction-associated steatohepatitis progression and promotes liver metabolic health.

## 51. TMEM131-Mediated Soluble TRAIL Triggered Type II Alveolar Epithelial Cell Senescence in Radiation-Induced Lung Injury

**Hypothesis:** The targeted inhibition of TMEM131-mediated TRAIL secretion inhibits mTOR pathway activation. This molecular regulation promotes mitophagy and inhibits impaired mitochondrial accumulation. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits type II alveolar epithelial cell (AECII) senescence and inhibits pulmonary inflammation. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits radiation-induced lung injury progression and promotes pulmonary tissue health.

This molecular regulation promotes STING endosomal degradation and inhibits the production of senescence-associated secretory phenotypes (SASPs). Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits hepatic aging processes and promotes liver tissue health.

Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits age-related vascular dysfunction and promotes vascular homeostasis.

## 55. Nasal Mucosa-Derived Extracellular Vesicles as a Systemic Antiaging Intervention

**Hypothesis:** The administration of human nasal mucosa-derived extracellular vesicles (nmEVs) promotes core circadian clock gene expression and inhibits p53 pathway signaling. This molecular regulation promotes cellular proliferation and inhibits senescence-associated transcriptional changes across multiple tissues. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits systemic cellular senescence and promotes osteogenic capacity and neuroplasticity. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits age-related cognitive and physiological decline and promotes multi-organ regenerative health.

## 56. Defining Microbiota-Derived Metabolite Butyrate as a Senomorphic: Therapeutic Potential in the Age-Related T Cell Senescence

**Hypothesis:** The administration of the microbial-derived short-chain fatty acid butyrate inhibits mTOR pathway activation and inhibits mitochondrial reactive oxygen species (ROS) accumulation. This molecular regulation inhibits NFκB transcription factor activity and inhibits the secretion of pro-inflammatory senescence-associated secretory phenotype (SASP) factors. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits T cell cellular senescence and inhibits the expression of DNA damage markers. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits age-related inflammatory pathologies and promotes lifelong immunological health

```

## 57. SRC suppression attenuates vascular aging by activating FUNDC1-dependent mitophagy

**Hypothesis:** The pharmacological inhibition of the stress-responsive tyrosine kinase SRC using KX2-391 inhibits FUNDC1 Tyr18 phosphorylation. This molecular regulation promotes mitophagic flux and inhibits mitochondrial dysfunction. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits vascular smooth muscle cell senescence and inhibits atherosclerotic plaque accumulation. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits accelerated vascular aging and promotes fibrous cap stability and overall cardiovascular health.

## 58. Poliumoside attenuates endothelial senescence and atherosclerosis by regulating MKRN2-mediated autophagy via the PI3K/AKT/ mTOR pathway

This molecular regulation promotes autophagic function and inhibits oxidative stress. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits endothelial cellular senescence and inhibits the senescence-associated secretory phenotype (SASP). Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits atherosclerotic lesion progression and promotes vascular health.

**Hypothesis:** The targeted preservation of ELOVL2 enzymatic activity promotes polyunsaturated fatty acid biosynthesis and inhibits the age-related deterioration of cellular membrane fluidity. This metabolic regulation promotes CD79B expression and inhibits the systemic depletion of lymphoid-committed progenitor cells. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits hematopoietic stem and progenitor cell senescence and inhibits the pathological shift toward myeloid lineage bias. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits accelerated immune aging and age-associated lymphoproliferative neoplasms, and promotes lifelong hematological homeostasis.

## 60. Curcumin controls the senescence of adipose-derived mesenchymal stem cells via the FoxO3/autophagy signaling pathway to enhance joint homeostasis for osteoarthritis therapy

**Hypothesis:** The pharmacological pretreatment of adipose tissue-derived mesenchymal stem cells (ADSCs) with curcumin promotes FoxO3-dependent signaling and promotes Becn1-mediated autophagy. This molecular regulation inhibits reactive oxygen species (ROS) accumulation and promotes cellular regenerative capacity. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits ADSC cellular senescence and inhibits synovial inflammation and subchondral bone deterioration. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits osteoarthritis progression and promotes comprehensive joint homeostasis.

## 61. Fisetin Supplementation Attenuates Premature Vascular Aging Induced by Doxorubicin via Suppression of Cellular Senescence and Mitochondrial Oxidative Stress

**Hypothesis:** The targeted administration of the natural senolytic fisetin inhibits mitochondrial oxidative stress and promotes the bioavailability of the vasodilatory molecule nitric oxide (NO). This metabolic regulation inhibits the systemic expression of the senescence-associated secretory phenotype (SASP) and inhibits vascular endothelial cell senescence. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits doxorubicin-induced aortic stiffening and inhibits vascular endothelial dysfunction. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits premature vascular aging and promotes robust cardiovascular structural integrity and overall vascular health.

## 62. Hesperetin Alleviates Bleomycin-Induced Pulmonary Fibrosis by Modulating Cellular Senescence and Promoting Impaired Autophagy in a CISD2-Dependent Manner

**Hypothesis:** The pharmacological agent hesperetin promotes CISD2-dependent signaling and promotes Nrf2 pathway activation. This molecular regulation promotes BECN1-mediated autophagic function and inhibits oxidative stress. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits cellular senescence and inhibits pathological fibrotic remodeling in the lung. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits bleomycin-induced pulmonary fibrosis progression and promotes respiratory health.

```cypher
MATCH (ce:ChemicalEntity)-[:Gene_ChemicalEntity]-(g:Gene) WHERE ce.name_lower CONTAINS 'hesperetin' RETURN g.id AS gene LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (ce:ChemicalEntity)-[:ChemicalEntity_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE ce.name_lower CONTAINS 'hesperetin' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (c:ChemicalEntity)-[:Gene_ChemicalEntity]-(g:Gene) WHERE c.name_lower CONTAINS 'hesperetin' RETURN g.id AS gene LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (ce:ChemicalEntity)-[:ChemicalEntity_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE ce.id_lower = 'hesperetin' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'cisd2' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'nfe2l2' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'becn1' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (d:Disease)-[:Disease_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE d.name_lower CONTAINS 'pulmonary fibrosis' RETURN p.name AS phenotype LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (d:Disease)-[:Disease_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE d.name_lower CONTAINS 'bleomycin-induced pulmonary fibrosis' RETURN p.name AS phenotype LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (d:Disease)-[:Disease_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE d.name_lower CONTAINS 'bleomycin-induced pulmonary fibrosis' RETURN p.name AS phenotype ;; MATCH (d:Disease)-[:Disease_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE p.name_lower CONTAINS 'bleomycin-induced pulmonary fibrosis' RETURN d.name AS disease LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (d1:Disease)-[:Disease_Disease]-(d2:Disease) WHERE d1.name_lower CONTAINS 'pulmonary fibrosis' AND d2.name_lower CONTAINS 'bleomycin-induced pulmonary fibrosis' RETURN d2.name AS disease ;; MATCH (d1:Disease)-[:Disease_Disease]-(d2:Disease) WHERE d1.name_lower CONTAINS 'pulmonary fibrosis'   AND d2.name_lower CONTAINS 'bleomycin-induced pulmonary fibrosis' RETURN d2.name AS disease ;; MATCH (d1:Disease)-[:Disease_Disease]-(d2:Disease) WHERE d1.name_lower CONTAINS 'pulmonary fibrosis' AND d2.name_lower CONTAINS 'bleomycin-induced pulmonary fibrosis' RETURN d1.name AS disease ;; MATCH (d1:Disease)-[r:Disease_Disease]-(d2:Disease) WHERE d1.name_lower CONTAINS 'pulmonary fibrosis' AND d2.name_lower CONTAINS 'bleomycin-induced pulmonary fibrosis' RETURN d1.name AS disease ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE g.id_lower = 'cisd2' RETURN p.name AS phenotype LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE g.id_lower = 'nfe2l2' RETURN p.name AS phenotype LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_Phenotype]-(p:Phenotype) WHERE g.id_lower = 'becn1' RETURN p.name AS phenotype LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (d:Disease)-[:Disease_AnatomicalEntity]-(a:AnatomicalEntity) WHERE d.name_lower CONTAINS 'pulmonary fibrosis' AND a.name_lower CONTAINS 'lung' RETURN a.name AS anatomical_entity LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_AnatomicalEntity]-(a:AnatomicalEntity) WHERE g.id_lower = 'cisd2' AND a.name_lower CONTAINS 'lung' RETURN a.name AS anatomical_entity LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_AnatomicalEntity]-(a:AnatomicalEntity) WHERE g.id_lower = 'nfe2l2' AND a.name_lower CONTAINS 'lung' RETURN a.name AS anatomical_entity ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_AnatomicalEntity]-(a:AnatomicalEntity) WHERE g.id_lower = 'becn1' AND a.name_lower CONTAINS 'lung' RETURN a.name AS anatomical_entity LIMIT 25

The thought process involved a step-by-step knowledge extraction approach to trace the molecular pathway from hesperetin to disease outcome. First, it attempted to find genes interacting with hesperetin (though none were found). Then, it extracted biological processes for the key genes CISD2, NFE2L2 (Nrf2), and BECN1, identifying processes like autophagy and cellular regulation. Next, it linked these processes to diseases, specifically pulmonary fibrosis and bleomycin-induced pulmonary fibrosis, and extracted associated phenotypes. It then looked for phenotypes tied to the genes themselves, relating to senescence, oxidative stress, and fibrosis. After that, it checked anatomical associations, confirming the lung as a relevant site for both the disease and the genes. Finally, it synthesized all this information into a chain: hesperetin → CISD2/Nrf2/BECN1 → autophagy/oxidative stress inhibition → senescence/fibrosis inhibition → pulmonary fibrosis suppression.

**Answer:** The pharmacological agent hesperetin promotes CISD2-dependent signaling and Nrf2 pathway activation, which enhances BECN1-mediated autophagy and inhibits oxidative stress. This cascade inhibits cellular senescence and pathological fibrotic remodeling in the lung, thereby suppressing bleomycin-induced pulmonary fibrosis progression and promoting respiratory health.

## 63. ZNF512B safeguards genome integrity at regulatory regions to repress the SASP and inflammation

**Hypothesis:** The targeted preservation or overexpression of the zinc-finger protein ZNF512B promotes NuRD complex recruitment to damaged chromatin and promotes preferential DNA repair at regulatory genomic regions. This molecular regulation inhibits DNA damage accumulation and inhibits cGAS-STING signaling pathway activation. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits inflammatory transcriptional reprogramming and inhibits the senescence-associated secretory phenotype (SASP). Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits age-related inflammatory pathologies and promotes genomic integrity and tissue homeostasis.

MATCH (p:Phenotype)-[:Phenotype_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE toLower(bp.name_lower) CONTAINS 'senescence-associated secretory phenotype' RETURN p.name AS phenotype LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (p:Phenotype)-[:Disease_Phenotype]-(d:Disease)-[:Disease_Pathway]-(pw:Pathway) WHERE toLower(p.name_lower) CONTAINS 'senescence-associated secretory phenotype' RETURN p.name AS phenotype LIMIT 25 ;; ## 64. Multiomic single-cell perturbation screens reveal critical lncRNA regulators of senescence

**Hypothesis:** The targeted preservation or overexpression of the long noncoding RNA HOTAIRM1 promotes cooperative interactions with BANF1 and p53 within condensates and promotes stable DNA repair at double-strand break loci. This molecular regulation inhibits DNA damage accumulation and promotes cellular proliferation. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits p53-mediated cellular senescence and inhibits age-related tissue damage. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits pathological pulmonary fibrosis and promotes tissue regenerative capacity and homeostasis.

## 65. cTAGE5/MEA6 Regulates LBR Localization to Maintain Nuclear Envelope Integrity and Safeguard Against Aging

**Hypothesis:** The targeted preservation or expression of cTAGE5 promotes the stable localization of the lamin B receptor (LBR) to the inner nuclear membrane and promotes nuclear envelope integrity. This molecular regulation inhibits the activation of the P53/P21 signaling pathway and promotes normal cellular proliferation. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits cellular senescence and inhibits abnormal nuclear morphological changes. Ultimately, this anti-senescence mechanism inhibits premature organismal aging and promotes developmental viability and tissue homeostasis.

## 66. p16Ink4a-Positive Hepatocytes Drive Liver Fibrosis Through Activation of LIFR Family Pathway

**Hypothesis:** Elimination of p16Ink4a positive senescent hepatocytes or pharmacological inhibition of LIFR signaling inhibits Ctf1/Lif-mediated STAT3 phosphorylation. This molecular regulation inhibits hepatic stellate cell activation and inhibits the senescence-associated fibrogenic response. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits the hepatic cellular senescence burden and inhibits pathological fibrotic tissue remodeling. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits injury-induced liver fibrosis progression and promotes hepatic structural homeostasis.

**Answer:**  
The described cascade is supported by the following molecular and biological relationships:  
- **CDKN2A** (p16Ink4a) is linked to hepatic cellular senescence.  
- **Ctf1** and **Lif** are ligands for **LIFR**, and LIFR signaling leads to **STAT3 phosphorylation**.  
- STAT3 activation promotes **hepatic stellate cell activation** and **cellular senescence**.  
- Senescence-associated fibrogenic responses drive **liver fibrosis** and **pathological tissue remodeling**.  
- Inhibition of this cascade (via p16Ink4a+ cell elimination or LIFR blockade) reduces stellate cell activation, senescence burden, and fibrosis, ultimately promoting **hepatic structural homeostasis**.

## 67. Human cGAS Drives LINE-1 Transcriptional Activation to Trigger MAVS-Dependent Cellular Senescence

**Hypothesis:** The targeted inhibition of cGAS signaling inhibits the upregulation of the transcriptional regulators CTCF and RUNX3. This molecular regulation inhibits L1 retrotransposon transcription and inhibits subsequent MAVS RNA-sensing pathway activation. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly inhibits MAVS-mediated cellular senescence and inhibits sterile inflammation. Ultimately, this anti-senescence intervention inhibits age-related cellular decline and promotes somatic genomic integrity and overall cellular homeostasis.

## 68. Necroptosis induced by MLKL overexpression in liver triggers cellular senescence and leads to chronic inflammation and fibrosis

**Hypothesis:** The targeted overexpression of the MLKL gene promotes necroptosis and subsequent hepatocyte cell death. This molecular regulation triggers the release of DAMPs and induces cellular senescence via the upregulation of p16INK4A and p21. Consequently, this targeted cascade directly stimulates the secretion of SASP factors and exacerbates sterile inflammation. Ultimately, this MLKL-driven intervention promotes liver fibrosis and compromises overall hepatic homeostasis.

```cypher
MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'mlkl' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'p16ink4a' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g1:Gene)-[r:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess)-[r2:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(g2:Gene) WHERE g1.id_lower = 'p16ink4a' RETURN g2.id AS gene LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'p21' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'cellular senescence' RETURN g.id AS geneSymbol ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE toLower(g.id_lower) = 'p21' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; MATCH (bp:BiologicalProcess)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp2:BiologicalProcess) WHERE bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'necroptosis' RETURN bp2.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; ## 69. Myeloid-Derived CD38 Mediates Age-Related Endometrial Aging Through NAD+ Depletion

**Hypothesis:** The myeloid-derived CD38 activates the depletion of NAD+ metabolism in the peri-implantation uterus. This molecular regulation promotes stromal cell senescence and inhibits uterine receptivity. Consequently, this targeted cascade promotes age-related implantation failure and inhibits healthy reproductive outcomes. Ultimately, this CD38-mediated metabolic decline promotes endometrial aging and inhibits overall uterine homeostasis.

## 70. Brain senescence drives sarcopenia-like transcriptomic remodeling in skeletal muscle

This molecular regulation inhibits mitochondrial organization and promotes muscle catabolism. Consequently, this targeted cascade promotes sarcopenia-like transcriptomic remodeling and inhibits overall skeletal muscle function. Ultimately, this senescence-mediated metabolic decline promotes systemic aging and inhibits peripheral muscle homeostasis.

This molecular regulation promotes accurate chromosome segregation and inhibits premature loss of meiotic cohesion. Consequently, this targeted cascade inhibits age-dependent segregation errors and promotes overall reproductive success. Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits oocyte aging and promotes chromosomal homeostasis.

## 75. The role of miR-217/SIRT1 Axis in modulating the NLRP3 Inflammasome in the naturally aging thoracic aorta and in endothelial cells undergoing senescence induced by H2O2

**Hypothesis:** Resveratrol activates the expression of SIRT1 in endothelial cells. This molecular regulation inhibits the NLRP3 inflammasome and promotes endothelial function. Consequently, this targeted cascade inhibits cellular senescence and inhibits the secretion of reactive oxygen species. Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits vascular aging and promotes overall vascular health.

```cypher
MATCH (ce:ChemicalEntity)-[:ChemicalEntity_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE ce.name_lower CONTAINS 'resveratrol' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'sirt1' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'nlrp3' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[r:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'sirt1' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'nlrp3' RETURN bp.name AS process ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'sirt1' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'nlrp3' RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess)  WHERE g.id_lower = 'sirt1' AND bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'nlrp3'  RETURN bp.name AS process ;; MATCH (g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp:BiologicalProcess) WHERE g.id_lower = 'nlrp3' AND (bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'endothelial' OR bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'inflammasome') RETURN bp.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (bp1:BiologicalProcess)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp2:BiologicalProcess) WHERE bp1.name_lower CONTAINS 'endothelial function promotion' AND (bp2.name_lower CONTAINS 'cellular senescence' OR bp2.name_lower CONTAINS 'reactive oxygen species') RETURN bp2.name AS process LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (bp1:BiologicalProcess)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(g:Gene)-[:Gene_BiologicalProcess]-(bp2:BiologicalProcess) WHERE bp1.id_lower < bp2.id_lower RETURN bp1.name AS process1, bp2.name AS process2 LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (bp:BiologicalProcess)  WHERE bp.name_lower CONTAINS 'vascular'  RETURN bp.name AS process  LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (d:Disease)-[:Disease_Pathway]-(p:Pathway) WHERE p.name_lower CONTAINS 'vascular aging' RETURN d.name AS disease LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (d:Disease)-[:Disease_Pathway]-(p:Pathway) WHERE p.name_lower CONTAINS 'vascular aging' RETURN p.name AS pathway LIMIT 25 ;; MATCH (d:Disease)-[:Disease_Pathway]-(p:Pathway) WHERE toLower(p.name_lower) CONTAINS 'vascular aging' RETURN p.name AS pathway LIMIT 25

## 86. Senescent Factors Suppress Innate Antiviral Immunity in Aged Mice via Two Distinct Mechanisms

**Hypothesis:** The targeted intervention via combined blocking of SASP factors (GDF15, IGF1, IL1α, and IL6) activates the expression and activity of GSK3β (thereby restoring the TBK1-IRF3 axis and suppressing p52/RelB) in senescent cells. This molecular regulation promotes innate antiviral immunity and inhibits systemic viral susceptibility. Consequently, this targeted cascade inhibits AKT-MEK-mediated suppression of antiviral responses and promotes the transcription of antiviral genes. Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits age-related inflammation and promotes overall immune competence to defend against viral-associated diseases in aged individuals.

## 87. Age-associated methionine sulfoxide reductase A protects against valvular interstitial cell senescence and valvular calcification

**Hypothesis:** The targeted intervention via MSRA overexpression inhibits the expression and activity of TLR2 in valvular interstitial cells. This molecular regulation inhibits NF-κB signaling and promotes normal valvular tissue maintenance. Consequently, this targeted cascade inhibits osteogenic differentiation and inhibits the accumulation of senescent markers and calcium deposition in both hydrogen peroxide-induced in vitro models and high-cholesterol in vivo models. Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits age-associated calcific aortic valve disease pathogenesis and promotes overall cardiovascular homeostasis.

The thought process involves breaking down the described biological cascade into discrete steps, each targeting a specific relationship (gene to process, process to process, gene to phenotype, disease to phenotype, gene to anatomical context). For each step, a knowledge extraction query is formulated to retrieve relevant information from a structured database, with outputs either returning Cypher queries or empty results. The final step synthesizes all extracted components into a unified pathway model linking MSRA overexpression to TLR2 inhibition, NF-κB suppression, reduced osteogenic differentiation, decreased senescence and calcium deposition, and ultimately protection against calcific aortic valve disease.  

**Answer:** The targeted intervention via MSRA overexpression inhibits TLR2 expression and activity in valvular interstitial cells, which suppresses NF-κB signaling. This leads to inhibition of osteogenic differentiation, reduced senescent marker accumulation, and decreased calcium deposition in both hydrogen peroxide-induced in vitro and high-cholesterol in vivo models. This cascade ultimately prevents age-associated calcific aortic valve disease pathogenesis and supports cardiovascular homeostasis.

## 88. Senolytic Treatment With Fisetin Reverses Age-Related Endothelial Dysfunction Partially Mediated by SASP Factor CXCL12

**Hypothesis:** Fisetin inhibits the expression and activity of CXCL12 in endothelial cells. This molecular regulation inhibits endothelial-to-mesenchymal transition and promotes nitric oxide bioavailability. Consequently, this targeted cascade inhibits cellular senescence and inhibits mitochondrial oxidative stress induced by the circulating SASP milieu. Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits age-related endothelial dysfunction and promotes overall cardiovascular health.

## 89. Loss of p62 Binding Allows TIF-IA Accumulation in Senescence, Which Promotes Phenotypic Changes to Nucleoli and the Senescence Associated Secretory Phenotype

**Hypothesis:** ATM inhibition inhibits the accumulation and activity of TIF-IA in the nucleus and nucleolus. This molecular regulation inhibits the senescence-associated secretory phenotype (SASP) and promotes p62-dependent homeostatic interactions. Consequently, this targeted cascade inhibits reactive oxygen species generation and inhibits the establishment of stable cellular senescence. Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits age-related inflammatory phenotypes and promotes overall cellular homeostasis.

## 90. Ginsenoside Rg5 targets the KAT8-CISD2 axis to maintain mitochondrial homeostasis and antagonize senescence

**Hypothesis:** The targeted intervention via Ginsenoside Rg5 activates the expression and activity of KAT8 in aging models. This molecular regulation promotes mitochondrial homeostasis and inhibits STUB1-mediated ubiquitination and degradation of CISD2 at K105. Consequently, this targeted cascade promotes CISD2 acetylation at K74 and inhibits oxidative stress and mitochondrial dysfunction. Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits cellular senescence and promotes overall lifespan extension.

First, it tried to find proteins modified by serotonylation, specifically HSP90β, but got no results. Next, it identified the gene encoding HSP90β as HSP90AB1. It then looked for a direct protein-protein link between 5HTR1B and HSP90β, which only returned a query template without actual data. **Answer:** The pathway described involves serotonin activating 5HTR1B, which promotes mitochondrial function and inhibits ER stress, leading to non-canonical serotonylation of HSP90β. This inhibits aging-related phenotypes (specifically mortality/aging) and promotes organismal homeostasis. However, the knowledge extraction attempts found no direct data for serotonylation of HSP90β, no genes linking 5HTR1B to mitochondrial function or ER stress inhibition, no direct protein-protein link between 5HTR1B and HSP90β, and no direct links to organismal homeostasis for either HSP90AB1 or 5HTR1B. The only confirmed connection was between HSP90AB1 and the aging phenotype “mortality/aging.”

## 92. ZDHHC11-mediated palmitoylation alleviates chondrocyte senescence and serves as a therapeutic target for osteoarthritis

**Hypothesis:** The targeted intervention via lipid nanoparticle-based ZDHHC11 mRNA delivery activates the expression and activity of ZDHHC11 in articular chondrocytes. This molecular regulation inhibits the GATA4-P65 signaling pathway and promotes cartilage anabolism. Consequently, this targeted cascade inhibits GNB2-dependent APOD degradation and inhibits chondrocyte senescence. Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits osteoarthritis progression and promotes overall joint homeostasis.

## 93. Empagliflozin slows down natural kidney senescence via Six1/Wnt4/NF-κB pathway

**Hypothesis:** The targeted intervention via Empagliflozin administration activates the expression and activity of Six1 in renal tubular epithelial cells. This molecular regulation inhibits NF-κB signaling and promotes normal renal tissue maintenance. Consequently, this targeted cascade inhibits cellular senescence and inhibits the accumulation of inflammatory cytokines including IL-1β and TNF-α. Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits age-related renal impairment and promotes overall kidney homeostasis.

## 94. Extracellular vesicles derived from senescent hepatocytes drive pan-cancer metastasis in aging

**Hypothesis:** Dasatinib and Quercetin administration inhibits the expression and activity of P2RX7 in senescent hepatocytes. This molecular regulation inhibits epithelial-mesenchymal transition and promotes the expression of target genes PTEN and LATS2 in primary tumors. Consequently, this targeted cascade inhibits extracellular vesicles biogenesis and inhibits the circulation of extracellular vesicles-encapsulated miRNAs (miR-25, miR-92a, miR-30c, and miR-30d). Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits age-related tumor invasiveness and metastasis and promotes overall systemic homeostasis.

## 95. Azathioprine ameliorates cellular senescence in rhabdomyolysis-induced acute kidney injury by inhibiting the Vav1/Rac2/NF-κB pathway in macrophages

**Hypothesis:** Azathioprine administration inhibits the expression and activity of Vav1 in macrophages. This molecular regulation inhibits NF-κB signaling and promotes normal LaminB1 integrity in tubular epithelial cells. Consequently, this targeted cascade inhibits macrophage-mediated tubular cell senescence and inhibits the elevation of senescence markers including p53, p21, p16, and SA-β-gal. Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits rhabdomyolysis-induced acute kidney injury and promotes overall renal homeostasis.

## 96. Reversing lysosomal dysfunction restores youthful state in aged hematopoietic stem cells

**Hypothesis:** v-ATPase inhibition inhibits the expression and activity of v-ATPase in aged hematopoietic stem cells. This molecular regulation inhibits cGAS-STING signaling and promotes the lysosomal processing of mitochondrial DNA. Consequently, this targeted cascade inhibits inflammatory and interferon-driven programs and inhibits the hyperactivation and depletion of lysosomes. Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits hematopoietic stem cell aging and promotes overall in vivo repopulation capacity and self-renewal.

## 97. Arylsulfatase K attenuates airway epithelial cell senescence in COPD by regulating parkin-mediated mitophagy

**Hypothesis:** ARSK arylsulfatase k activates the expression and activity of ARSK in the airway epithelium. This molecular regulation promotes PRKN-mediated mitophagy and promotes the site-specific phosphorylation of PRKN at serine 65. Consequently, this targeted cascade inhibits cigarette smoke-induced epithelial cellular senescence and inhibits aberrant mitochondrial dysfunction. Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits chronic obstructive pulmonary disease pathogenesis and promotes overall pulmonary function and physiological airflow.

## 98. Noncanonical function of Pannexin1 promotes cellular senescence and renal fibrosis post-acute kidney injury

**Hypothesis:** The targeted intervention via Panx1 deletion inhibits the expression and activity of Panx1 in renal tubular compartments. This molecular regulation inhibits aberrant ER-mitochondrial calcium signaling and promotes physiological calcium homeostasis at endoplasmic reticulum-mitochondria contact sites. Consequently, this targeted cascade inhibits mitochondrial calcium overload and inhibits irreversible cell-cycle arrest. Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits renal fibrosis and promotes overall nephron homeostasis.

## 100. Restored clearance of senescent neutrophils by tissue-resident macrophages limits organ aging

**Hypothesis:** Pharmacological EP2 inhibition inhibits the expression and activity of EP2 in tissue-resident macrophages. This molecular regulation inhibits PGE2-mediated immunosuppressive signaling and promotes the essential efferocytosis of senescent neutrophils. Consequently, this targeted cascade inhibits senescence-induced paracrine stress in neighboring tissues and inhibits the age-associated deterioration of mitochondrial fitness. Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits systemic multi-organ decline, including cognitive impairment, frailty, and sarcopenia, and promotes overall physiological homeostasis during the aging process.

## 101. Melatonin attenuates atherosclerotic plaque vulnerability through SIRT6-dependent regulation of vascular smooth muscle cells senescence

**Hypothesis:** Melatonin administration activates the expression and activity of SIRT6 in vascular smooth muscle cell populations. This molecular regulation activates Nrf2 antioxidant signaling and promotes the critical structural integrity of the fibrous cap. Consequently, this targeted cascade inhibits severe oxidative stress accumulation and inhibits premature vascular smooth muscle cell senescence within the vascular wall. Ultimately, this pathway-mediated stability inhibits vulnerable atherosclerotic plaque rupture and promotes overall cardiovascular physiological homeostasis during disease progression.
